Remove quotes, commas, extra punctuation\n    text = text.replace('\"', '').replace(\"'\", '')\n\n    # Replace ampersands with 'and'\n    text = text.replace(' & ', ' and ')\n\n    # Remove multiple spaces\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n\n    # Strip leading/trailing spaces\n    return text.strip()\n```\n\n### Singular/Plural Normalization\n```python\ndef singularize_simple(word):\n    \"\"\"Simple singularization for common patterns\"\"\"\n    if word.endswith('ies'):\n        return word[:-3] + 'y'\n    elif word.endswith('es'):\n        return word[:-2]\n    elif word.endswith('s') and not word.endswith('ss'):\n        return word[:-1]\n    return word\n\ndef normalize_plurality(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category names to singular form\"\"\"\n    words = text.split()\n    # Usually singularize the last word (main noun)\n    if words:\n        words[-1] = singularize_simple(words[-1])\n    return ' '.join(words)\n```\n\n### Synonym Mapping\n```python\nCATEGORY_SYNONYMS = {\n    'apparel': 'clothing',\n    'footwear': 'shoes',\n    'eyewear': 'glasses',\n    'automobile': 'automotive',\n    'mobile': 'phone',\n    'laptop': 'computer',\n    'beverage': 'drink',\n}\n\ndef apply_synonyms(text):\n    \"\"\"Replace synonyms with standard terms\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    for synonym, standard in CATEGORY_SYNONYMS.items():\n        text_lower = text_lower.replace(synonym, standard)\n    return text_lower.title()\n```\n\n### Abbreviation Expansion\n```python\nABBREVIATIONS = {\n    'pcs': 'pieces',\n    'w/': 'with',\n    'wo/': 'without',\n    'qty': 'quantity',\n    'oz': 'ounce',\n    'lb': 'pound',\n}\n\ndef expand_abbreviations(text):\n    \"\"\"Expand common abbreviations\"\"\"\n    for abbr, full in ABBREVIATIONS.items():\n        text = re.sub(r'\\b' + abbr + r'\\b', full, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return text\n```\n\n## Advanced Normalization\n\n### Word Order Standardization\n```python\ndef standardize_word_order(text):\n    \"\"\"Put words in a standard order (e.g., 'men clothing' -> 'clothing men')\"\"\"\n    # Define category structure: [item type] [modifier] [subcategory]\n    modifiers = ['women', 'men', 'kids', 'baby', 'adult', 'professional', 'outdoor']\n\n    words = text.lower().split()\n    modifier_words = [w for w in words if w in modifiers]\n    other_words = [w for w in words if w not in modifiers]\n\n    # Reconstruct: main category first, then modifiers\n    return ' '.join(other_words + modifier_words).title()\n```\n\n### Removing Redundancy\n```python\ndef remove_redundant_words(text):\n    \"\"\"Remove redundant words like 'products', 'items', 'accessories' at the end\"\"\"\n    redundant = ['products', 'items', 'accessories', 'supplies', 'goods']\n    words = text.lower().split()\n\n    # Remove only if it's the last word and there are other words\n    if len(words) > 1 and words[-1] in redundant:\n        words = words[:-1]\n\n    return ' '.join(words).title()\n```\n\n## Similarity and Deduplication\n\n### Calculating Text Similarity\n```python\ndef jaccard_similarity(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0\n\n    intersection = len(words1.intersection(words2))\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n```\n\n### Full Normalization Pipeline\n```python\ndef normalize_category_name(text, use_pipe_separator=False):\n    \"\"\"Complete normalization pipeline\"\"\"\n    # Basic cleaning\n    text = clean_category_name(text)\n\n    # Expand abbreviations\n    text = expand_abbreviations(text)\n\n    # Apply synonyms\n    text = apply_synonyms(text)\n\n    # Remove redundancy\n    text = remove_redundant_words(text)\n\n    # Standardize case\n    text = smart_title_case(text)\n\n    # Optional: use pipe separator\n    if use_pipe_separator:\n        # Split and rejoin with pipe (for multi-word categories)\n        words = text.split()\n        if len(words) > 1:\n            text = ' | '.join(words)\n\n    return text\n```\n\n## Usage Example\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Apply normalization to dataframe\ndf['normalized_category'] = df['category_name'].apply(normalize_category_name)\n\n# Find similar categories\ndef find_similar_categories(df, threshold=0.7):\n    \"\"\"Find categories with high similarity\"\"\"\n    categories = df['normalized_category'].unique()\n    similar_pairs = []\n\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            similarity = jaccard_similarity(cat1, cat2)\n            if similarity >= threshold:\n                similar_pairs.append((cat1, cat2, similarity))\n\n    return similar_pairs\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":159,"cache_read_input_tokens":22372,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":159,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bb60abe-de6b-48dd-a5f6-c0523592c4fa","uuid":"2410daf0-76c0-4f65-800c-fe6cddfd2a08"}

Build hierarchy from bottom up\n        for i in range(len(levels)):\n            if i == 0:\n                taxonomy[f'level_{i+1}'][None].add(levels[i])\n            else:\n                parent = levels[i-1]\n                taxonomy[f'level_{i+1}'][parent].add(levels[i])\n\n    return taxonomy\n```\n\n### Top-Down Taxonomy Building\nStart with broad categories and split into subcategories.\n\n```python\ndef cluster_categories_by_keywords(categories, keywords_dict):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories into top-level groups based on keywords\"\"\"\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for category in categories:\n        matched = False\n        for cluster_name, keywords in keywords_dict.items():\n            if any(keyword.lower() in category.lower() for keyword in keywords):\n                clusters[cluster_name].append(category)\n                matched = True\n                break\n\n        if not matched:\n            clusters['Other'].append(category)\n\n    return clusters\n```\n\n### TF-IDF Based Clustering\n```python\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nfrom sklearn.cluster import KMeans\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters=15):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories using TF-IDF and K-Means\"\"\"\n    # Vectorize category names\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=100, stop_words='english')\n    X = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=42)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Group categories by cluster\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n    for category, label in zip(categories, labels):\n        clusters[f'cluster_{label}'].append(category)\n\n    return clusters, labels\n```\n\n### Naming Clusters\n```python\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, max_words=3):\n    \"\"\"Name a cluster based on most common words in categories\"\"\"\n    # Tokenize all categories\n    all_words = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        words = cat.lower().split()\n        all_words.extend(words)\n\n    # Find most common words\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n\n    # Filter out very common/stop words\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on'}\n    common_words = [word for word, count in word_counts.most_common(10)\n                    if word not in stop_words]\n\n    # Take top words\n    cluster_name = ' | '.join(common_words[:max_words])\n    return cluster_name.title()\n\ndef name_cluster_by_prefix(categories):\n    \"\"\"Name cluster based on longest common prefix\"\"\"\n    if not categories:\n        return \"Uncategorized\"\n\n    # Find common words in first position\n    first_words = [cat.split()[0] if cat.split() else '' for cat in categories]\n    word_counts = Counter(first_words)\n\n    # Use most common first word\n    if word_counts:\n        most_common = word_counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\n        return most_common.title()\n\n    return \"Mixed\"\n```\n\n## Building Balanced Hierarchies\n\n### Pyramid Structure Validation\n```python\ndef validate_pyramid_structure(taxonomy_df, target_ratios=[1, 3, 10, 30, 100]):\n    \"\"\"Check if taxonomy follows a pyramid structure\"\"\"\n    level_counts = {}\n\n    for i in range(1, 6):\n        level_col = f'level_{i}'\n        if level_col in taxonomy_df.columns:\n            unique_count = taxonomy_df[level_col].nunique()\n            level_counts[i] = unique_count\n\n    print(\"Taxonomy Structure:\")\n    for level, count in level_counts.items():\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories\")\n\n    # Check ratios\n    for i in range(1, len(level_counts)):\n        if i+1 in level_counts:\n            ratio = level_counts[i+1] / level_counts[i]\n            print(f\"  Ratio L{i+1}/L{i}: {ratio:.2f}\")\n\n    return level_counts\n```\n\n### Rebalancing Clusters\n```python\ndef rebalance_clusters(clusters, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Split large clusters and merge small ones\"\"\"\n    balanced = {}\n    small_clusters = []\n\n    for name, items in clusters.items():\n        if len(items) < min_size:\n            small_clusters.extend(items)\n        elif len(items) > max_size:\n            # Split large cluster\n            mid = len(items) // 2\n            balanced[f\"{name}_A\"] = items[:mid]\n            balanced[f\"{name}_B\"] = items[mid:]\n        else:\n            balanced[name] = items\n\n    # Merge small clusters\n    if small_clusters:\n        balanced['Mixed'] = small_clusters\n\n    return balanced\n```\n\n## Hierarchical Category Assignment\n\n### Multi-Level Assignment\n```python\ndef assign_hierarchical_categories(df, level_clusters):\n    \"\"\"Assign categories at multiple levels\"\"\"\n    # Fill missing parent categories based on children\n        for idx, row in df.iterrows():\n            if pd.notna(row[current_col]) and pd.isna(row[parent_col]):\n                # Find parent from other rows with same child\n                parent = df[df[current_col] == row[current_col]][parent_col].mode()\n                if len(parent) > 0:\n                    df.at[idx, parent_col] = parent.iloc[0]\n\n    return df\n```\n\n## Complete Taxonomy Building Pipeline\n\n```python\ndef build_unified_taxonomy(category_lists, n_top_categories=15, max_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build a unified taxonomy from multiple category lists\n\n    Args:\n        category_lists: List of (source, category_path) tuples\n        n_top_categories: Number of top-level categories\n        max_depth: Maximum depth of taxonomy\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with unified taxonomy mapping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Category Similarity Metrics and Deduplication\n\n## Overview\nTechniques for measuring similarity between category names and detecting duplicates in taxonomy systems.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install pandas numpy scikit-learn\n# Optional for fuzzy matching\npip install fuzzywuzzy python-Levenshtein\n```\n\n## Basic Similarity Metrics\n\n### Jaccard Similarity\n```python\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Jaccard similarity based on word sets\n    Range: 0 (no overlap) to 1 (identical)\n    \"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n### Word Overlap Percentage\n```python\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate percentage of overlapping words\n    Returns overlap relative to smaller set\n    \"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n```\n\n### Cosine Similarity with TF-IDF\n```python\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nfrom sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n\ndef compute_tfidf_similarity(categories):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute pairwise cosine similarity using TF-IDF\n    Returns similarity matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer()\n    tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n\n    similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n    return similarity_matrix\n```\n\n### Levenshtein Distance\n```python\ndef levenshtein_distance(s1, s2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate edit distance between two strings\n    Lower is more similar\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(s1) < len(s2):\n        return levenshtein_distance(s2, s1)\n\n    if len(s2) == 0:\n        return len(s1)\n\n    previous_row = range(len(s2) + 1)\n    for i, c1 in enumerate(s1):\n        current_row = [i + 1]\n        for j, c2 in enumerate(s2):\n            insertions = previous_row[j + 1] + 1\n            deletions = current_row[j] + 1\n            substitutions = previous_row[j] + (c1 != c2)\n            current_row.append(min(insertions, deletions, substitutions))\n        previous_row = current_row\n\n    return previous_row[-1]\n\ndef normalized_levenshtein(s1, s2):\n    \"\"\"Normalized Levenshtein distance (0 to 1)\"\"\"\n    distance = levenshtein_distance(s1.lower(), s2.lower())\n    max_len = max(len(s1), len(s2))\n    return 1 - (distance / max_len) if max_len > 0 else 1.0\n```\n\n## Advanced Similarity Techniques\n\n### Fuzzy String Matching\n```python\nfrom fuzzywuzzy import fuzz\n\ndef fuzzy_match_score(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Multiple fuzzy matching scores\n    Returns dict with different similarity measures\n    \"\"\"\n    return {\n        'simple': fuzz.ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n        'partial': fuzz.partial_ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n        'token_sort': fuzz.token_sort_ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n        'token_set': fuzz.token_set_ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n    }\n```\n\n### Semantic Similarity (Keyword-Based)\n```python\ndef semantic_similarity(cat1, cat2, synonym_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate similarity considering synonyms\n\n    synonym_dict: {word: [synonym1, synonym2, ...]}\n    \"\"\"\n    def expand_with_synonyms(text, synonym_dict):\n        words = set(text.lower().split())\n        expanded = words.copy()\n        for word in words:\n            if word in synonym_dict:\n                expanded.update(synonym_dict[word])\n        return expanded\n\n    set1 = expand_with_synonyms(cat1, synonym_dict)\n    set2 = expand_with_synonyms(cat2, synonym_dict)\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n## Deduplication Strategies\n\n### Find Duplicate Categories\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef find_duplicates(categories, threshold=0.8, metric='jaccard'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find near-duplicate categories above similarity threshold\n\n    Returns: List of (cat1, cat2, similarity) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    duplicates = []\n\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            if metric == 'jaccard':\n                sim = cat2)\n\n            if sim >= threshold:\n                duplicates.append((cat1, cat2, sim))\n\n    return duplicates\n```\n\n### Merge Similar Categories\n```python\ndef categories = df[category_col].unique()\n\n    # Build equivalence groups\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    groups = defaultdict(set)\n    category_to_group = {}\n    group_id = 0\n\n    for cat in categories:\n        if cat not in category_to_group:\n            # Start new group\n            groups[group_id].add(cat)\n            category_to_group[cat] = group_id\n            group_id += 1\n\n    # Find similar pairs and merge groups\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            if jaccard_similarity(cat1, cat2) >=    # Merge group2 into group1\n                    for cat in groups[group2]:\n                        groups[group1].add(cat)\n                        category_to_group[cat] = group1\n                    del groups[group2]\n\n    # Choose representative for each group (most common or shortest)\n    mapping = {}\n    for group_id, cats in groups.items():\n        # Use shortest name as representative\n        representative = min(cats, key=len)\n        for cat in cats:\n            mapping[cat] = representative\n\n    # Apply mapping\n    df[f'{category_col}_merged'] = \"\"\"\n    Check if child category name overlaps too much with parent\n    Returns True if child should be renamed to avoid redundancy\n    \"\"\"\n    parent_words = set(parent.lower().split())\n    child_words = set(child.lower().split())\n\n    # Calculate overlap\n    overlap = len(parent_words & child_words)\n\n    # If child contains all parent words, it's redundant\n    if overlap == Remove quotes, commas, extra punctuation\n    text = text.replace('\"', '').replace(\"'\", '')\n\n    # Replace ampersands with 'and'\n    text = text.replace(' & ', ' and ')\n\n    # Remove multiple spaces\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n\n    # Strip leading/trailing spaces\n    return text.strip()\n```\n\n### Singular/Plural Normalization\n```python\ndef singularize_simple(word):\n    \"\"\"Simple singularization for common patterns\"\"\"\n    if word.endswith('ies'):\n        return word[:-3] + 'y'\n    elif word.endswith('es'):\n        return word[:-2]\n    elif word.endswith('s') and not word.endswith('ss'):\n        return word[:-1]\n    return word\n\ndef normalize_plurality(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category names to singular form\"\"\"\n    words = text.split()\n    # Usually singularize the last word (main noun)\n    if words:\n        words[-1] = singularize_simple(words[-1])\n    return ' '.join(words)\n```\n\n### Synonym Mapping\n```python\nCATEGORY_SYNONYMS = {\n    'apparel': 'clothing',\n    'footwear': 'shoes',\n    'eyewear': 'glasses',\n    'automobile': 'automotive',\n    'mobile': 'phone',\n    'laptop': 'computer',\n    'beverage': 'drink',\n}\n\ndef apply_synonyms(text):\n    \"\"\"Replace synonyms with standard terms\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    for synonym, standard in CATEGORY_SYNONYMS.items():\n        text_lower = text_lower.replace(synonym, standard)\n    return text_lower.title()\n```\n\n### Abbreviation Expansion\n```python\nABBREVIATIONS = {\n    'pcs': 'pieces',\n    'w/': 'with',\n    'wo/': 'without',\n    'qty': 'quantity',\n    'oz': 'ounce',\n    'lb': 'pound',\n}\n\ndef expand_abbreviations(text):\n    \"\"\"Expand common abbreviations\"\"\"\n    for abbr, full in ABBREVIATIONS.items():\n        text = re.sub(r'\\b' + abbr + r'\\b', full, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return text\n```\n\n## Advanced Normalization\n\n### Word Order Standardization\n```python\ndef standardize_word_order(text):\n    \"\"\"Put words in a standard order (e.g., 'men clothing' -> 'clothing men')\"\"\"\n    # Define category structure: [item type] [modifier] [subcategory]\n    modifiers = ['women', 'men', 'kids', 'baby', 'adult', 'professional', 'outdoor']\n\n    words = text.lower().split()\n    modifier_words = [w for w in words if w in modifiers]\n    other_words = [w for w in words if w not in modifiers]\n\n    # Reconstruct: main category first, then modifiers\n    return ' '.join(other_words + modifier_words).title()\n```\n\n### Removing Redundancy\n```python\ndef remove_redundant_words(text):\n    \"\"\"Remove redundant words like 'products', 'items', 'accessories' at the end\"\"\"\n    redundant = ['products', 'items', 'accessories', 'supplies', 'goods']\n    words = text.lower().split()\n\n    # Remove only if it's the last word and there are other words\n    if len(words) > 1 and words[-1] in redundant:\n        words = words[:-1]\n\n    return ' '.join(words).title()\n```\n\n## Similarity and Deduplication\n\n### Calculating Text Similarity\n```python\ndef jaccard_similarity(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1.intersection(set2))\n    union = len(set1.union(set2))\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0\n\ndef word_overlap_ratio(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0\n\n    intersection = len(words1.intersection(words2))\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n```\n\n### Full Normalization Pipeline\n```python\ndef normalize_category_name(text, use_pipe_separator=False):\n    \"\"\"Complete normalization pipeline\"\"\"\n    # Basic cleaning\n    text = clean_category_name(text)\n\n    # Expand abbreviations\n    text = expand_abbreviations(text)\n\n    # Apply synonyms\n    text = apply_synonyms(text)\n\n    # Remove redundancy\n    text = remove_redundant_words(text)\n\n    # Standardize case\n    text = smart_title_case(text)\n\n    # Optional: use pipe separator\n    if use_pipe_separator:\n        # Split and rejoin with pipe (for multi-word categories)\n        words = text.split()\n        if len(words) > 1:\n            text = ' | '.join(words)\n\n    return text\n```\n\n## Usage Example\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Apply normalization to dataframe\ndf['normalized_category'] = df['category_name'].apply(normalize_category_name)\n\n# Find similar categories\ndef find_similar_categories(df, threshold=0.7):\n    \"\"\"Find categories with high similarity\"\"\"\n    categories = df['normalized_category'].unique()\n    similar_pairs = []\n\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            similarity = jaccard_similarity(cat1, cat2)\n            if similarity >= threshold:\n                similar_pairs.append((cat1, cat2, similarity))\n\n    return similar_pairs\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Build hierarchy from bottom up\n        for i in range(len(levels)):\n            if i == 0:\n                taxonomy[f'level_{i+1}'][None].add(levels[i])\n            else:\n                parent = levels[i-1]\n                taxonomy[f'level_{i+1}'][parent].add(levels[i])\n\n    return taxonomy\n```\n\n### Top-Down Taxonomy Building\nStart with broad categories and split into subcategories.\n\n```python\ndef cluster_categories_by_keywords(categories, keywords_dict):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories into top-level groups based on keywords\"\"\"\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for category in categories:\n        matched = False\n        for cluster_name, keywords in keywords_dict.items():\n            if any(keyword.lower() in category.lower() for keyword in keywords):\n                clusters[cluster_name].append(category)\n                matched = True\n                break\n\n        if not matched:\n            clusters['Other'].append(category)\n\n    return clusters\n```\n\n### TF-IDF Based Clustering\n```python\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nfrom sklearn.cluster import KMeans\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters=15):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories using TF-IDF and K-Means\"\"\"\n    # Vectorize category names\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=100, stop_words='english')\n    X = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=42)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Group categories by cluster\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n    for category, label in zip(categories, labels):\n        clusters[f'cluster_{label}'].append(category)\n\n    return clusters, labels\n```\n\n### Naming Clusters\n```python\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, max_words=3):\n    \"\"\"Name a cluster based on most common words in categories\"\"\"\n    # Filter out very common/stop words\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on'}\n    common_words = [word for word, count in word_counts.most_common(10)\n                    if word not in stop_words]\n\n    # Find common words in first position\n    first_words = [cat.split()[0] if cat.split() else '' for cat in categories]\n    word_counts = Counter(first_words)\n\n    # Use most common first word\n    if word_counts:\n        most_common = word_counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\n        return most_common.title()\n\n    return \"Mixed\"\n```\n\n## Building Balanced Hierarchies\n\n### Pyramid Structure Validation\n```python\ndef validate_pyramid_structure(taxonomy_df, target_ratios=[1, 3, 10, 30, 100]):\n    \"\"\"Check if taxonomy follows a pyramid structure\"\"\"\n    level_counts = {}\n\n    for i in range(1, 6):\n        level_col = f'level_{i}'\n        if level_col in taxonomy_df.columns:\n            unique_count = taxonomy_df[level_col].nunique()\n            level_counts[i] = unique_count\n\n    print(\"Taxonomy Structure:\")\n    for level, count in level_counts.items():\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: {count} categories\")\n\n    # Category Similarity Metrics and Deduplication\n\n## Overview\nTechniques for measuring similarity between category names and detecting duplicates in taxonomy systems.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install pandas numpy scikit-learn\n# Optional for fuzzy matching\npip install fuzzywuzzy python-Levenshtein\n```\n\n## Basic Similarity Metrics\n\n### Jaccard Similarity\n```python\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Jaccard similarity based on word sets\n    Range: 0 (no overlap) to 1 (identical)\n    \"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n### Word Overlap Percentage\n```python\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate percentage of overlapping words\n    Returns overlap relative to smaller set\n    \"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n```\n\n### Cosine Similarity with TF-IDF\n```python\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nfrom sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n\ndef compute_tfidf_similarity(categories):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute pairwise cosine similarity using TF-IDF\n    Returns similarity matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer()\n    tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n\n    similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n    return similarity_matrix\n```\n\n### Levenshtein Distance\n```python\ndef levenshtein_distance(s1, s2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate edit distance between two strings\n    Lower is more similar\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(s1) < len(s2):\n        return levenshtein_distance(s2, s1)\n\n    if len(s2) == 0:\n        return len(s1)\n\n    previous_row = range(len(s2) + 1)\n    for i, c1 in enumerate(s1):\n        current_row = [i + 1]\n        for j, c2 in enumerate(s2):\n            insertions = previous_row[j + 1] + 1\n            deletions = current_row[j] + 1\n            substitutions = previous_row[j] + (c1 != c2)\n            current_row.append(min(insertions, deletions, substitutions))\n        previous_row = current_row\n\n    return previous_row[-1]\n\ndef normalized_levenshtein(s1, s2):\n    \"\"\"Normalized Levenshtein distance (0 to 1)\"\"\"\n    distance = levenshtein_distance(s1.lower(), s2.lower())\n    max_len = max(len(s1), len(s2))\n    return 1 - (distance / max_len) if max_len > 0 else 1.0\n```\n\n## Advanced Similarity Techniques\n\n### Fuzzy String Matching\n```python\nfrom fuzzywuzzy import fuzz\n\ndef fuzzy_match_score(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Multiple fuzzy matching scores\n    Returns dict with different similarity measures\n    \"\"\"\n    return {\n        'simple': fuzz.ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n        'partial': fuzz.partial_ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n        'token_sort': fuzz.token_sort_ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n        'token_set': fuzz.token_set_ratio(str1, str2) / 100,\n    }\n```\n\n### Semantic Similarity (Keyword-Based)\n```python\ndef semantic_similarity(cat1, cat2, synonym_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate similarity considering synonyms\n\n    synonym_dict: {word: [synonym1, synonym2, ...]}\n    \"\"\"\n    def expand_with_synonyms(text, synonym_dict):\n        words = set(text.lower().split())\n        expanded = words.copy()\n        for word in words:\n            if word in synonym_dict:\n                expanded.update(synonym_dict[word])\n        return expanded\n\n    set1 = expand_with_synonyms(cat1, synonym_dict)\n    set2 = expand_with_synonyms(cat2, synonym_dict)\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n## Deduplication Strategies\n\n### Find Duplicate Categories\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef find_duplicates(categories, threshold=0.8, metric='jaccard'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find near-duplicate categories above similarity threshold\n\n    Returns: List of (cat1, cat2, similarity) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    duplicates = []\n\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            if metric == 'jaccard':\n                sim = jaccard_similarity(cat1, cat2)\n            elif metric == 'word_overlap':\n                sim = word_overlap(cat1, cat2)\n            else:\n                sim = normalized_levenshtein(cat1, cat2)\n\n            if sim >= threshold:\n                duplicates.append((cat1, cat2, sim))\n\n    return duplicates\n```\n\n### Merge Similar Categories\n```python\ndef merge_similar_categories(df, category_col, threshold=0.8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge similar categories in a dataframe\n\n    Returns: DataFrame with merged categories and mapping dict\n    \"\"\"\n    categories = df[category_col].unique()\n\n    # Build equivalence groups\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    groups = defaultdict(set)\n    category_to_group = {}\n    group_id = 0\n\n    for cat in categories:\n        if cat not in category_to_group:\n            # Start new group\n            groups[group_id].add(cat)\n            category_to_group[cat] = group_id\n            group_id += 1\n\n    # Find similar pairs and merge groups\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            if jaccard_similarity(cat1, cat2) >= threshold:\n                # Merge groups\n                group1 = category_to_group[cat1]\n                group2 = category_to_group[cat2]\n\n                if group1 != group2:\n                    # Merge group2 into group1\n                    for cat in groups[group2]:\n                        groups[group1].add(cat)\n                        category_to_group[cat] = group1\n                    del groups[group2]\n\n    # Choose representative for each group (most common or shortest)\n    mapping = {}\n    for group_id, cats in groups.items():\n        # Use shortest name as representative\n        representative = min(cats, key=len)\n        for cat in cats:\n            mapping[cat] = representative\n\n    # Apply mapping\n    df[f'{category_col}_merged'] = df[category_col].map(mapping)\n\n    return df, mapping\n```\n\n## Parent-Child Overlap Detection\n\n### Check Parent-Child Redundancy\n```python\ndef check_parent_child_overlap(parent, child):\n child_words = set(child.lower().split())\n\n    # Calculate overlap\n    overlap = len(parent_words & child_words)\n\n    # If child contains all parent words, it's redundant\n    if overlap == Load CSV files from each source\n    2. Parse category paths into levels\n    3. Normalize text (case, punctuation, synonyms)\n    4. Count frequency of each category path\n    \"\"\"\n    import pandas as pd\n\n    all_data = []\n\n    for source, filepath in source_files.items():\n        df = pd.read_csv(filepath)\n        df['source'] = source\n\n        # Parse paths\n        df['levels'] = df['category_path'].apply(\n            lambda x: [normalize_text(level.strip())\n                      for level in x.split('>')]\n        )\n        df['depth'] = df['levels'].apply(len)\n\n        all_data.append(df)\n\n    combined = pd.concat(all_data, ignore_index=True)\n    return combined\n```\n\n### Phase 2: Build Initial Clusters at Each Level\n```python\ndef phase2_cluster_by_level(df, n_top=15):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster categories level by level\n\n    Level 1: Create 10-20 broad clusters using TF-IDF + K-Means\n    Level 2-5: Within each parent cluster, create subclusters\n    \"\"\"\n    from sklearn.cluster import KMeans\n    from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\n\n    # Level 1 clustering\n    level_1_cats = df['levels'].apply(lambda x: x[0] if len(x) > 0 else None)\n    level_1_cats = level_1_cats.dropna().unique()\n\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=50, stop_words='english')\n    X = vectorizer.fit_transform(level_1_cats)\n\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_top, random_state=42)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Create cluster mapping\n    cluster_map = {cat: f\"cluster_{label}\"\n                  for cat, label in zip(level_1_cats, labels)}\n\n    # Name clusters based on representative terms\n    for cluster_id in range(n_top):\n        cluster_cats = [cat for cat, label in zip(level_1_cats, labels)\n                       if label == cluster_id]\n        cluster_name = name_cluster(cluster_cats)\n\n        # Update mapping with meaningful name\n        for cat in cluster_cats:\n            cluster_map[cat] = cluster_name\n\n    return cluster_map\n```\n\n### Phase 3: Refine and Balance Clusters\n```python\ndef phase3_refine_clusters(clusters, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"\n    Refine clusters to ensure balanced pyramid structure\n\n    - Split clusters that are too large\n    - Merge clusters that are too small\n    - Ensure parent-child ratio is reasonable (3-10x)\n    \"\"\"\n    refined = {}\n\n    for cluster_name, items in clusters.items():\n        if len(items) < min_size:\n            # Mark for merging\n            refined.setdefault('_TO_MERGE', []).append((cluster_name, items))\n\n        elif len(items) > max_size:\n            # Split into subclusters\n            sub_clusters = split_cluster(items, n_splits=2)\n            for i, sub_items in enumerate(sub_clusters):\n                refined[f\"{cluster_name} | Type {i+1}\"] = sub_items\n\n        else:\n            refined[cluster_name] = items\n\n    # Merge small clusters\n    if '_TO_MERGE' in refined:\n        small_clusters = refined.pop('_TO_MERGE')\n        merged_items = []\n        for name, items in small_clusters:\n            merged_items.extend(items)\n\n        # Find best parent cluster for merged items\n        refined['Other'] = merged_items\n\n    return refined\n```\n\n### Phase 4: Name Categories Meaningfully\n```python\ndef phase4_name_categories(cluster_items, max_words=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate meaningful names for unified categories\n\n    Rules:\n    - Use pipe separator (|) for multi-word names\n    - Max 5 words\n    - Representative of 70%+ of subcategories\n    - No overlap with parent category name\n    \"\"\"\n    from collections import Counter\n\n    # Extract common words\n    all_words = []\n    for item in cluster_items:\n        words = item.lower().split()\n        all_words.extend(words)\n\n    # Get most common meaningful words\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'with', 'for', 'other', 'products'}\n    word_freq = Counter(all_words)\n    meaningful_words = [\n        word for word, count in word_freq.most_common(20)\n        if word not in stop_words and len(word) > 2\n    ]\n\n    # Check representativeness\n    selected_words = []\n    for word in meaningful_words[:max_words]:\n        # Count how many items contain this word\n        coverage = sum(1 for item in cluster_items if word in item.lower())\n        coverage_pct = coverage / len(cluster_items)\n\n        if coverage_pct >= 0.3:  # At least 30% coverage\n            selected_words.append(word.capitalize())\n\n        if len(selected_words) >= max_words:\n            break\n\n    # Create name with pipe separator\n    if selected_words:\n        return ' | '.join(selected_words[:max_words])\n    else:\n        # Fallback: use most common first word\n        first_words = [item.split()[0] for item in cluster_items if item.split()]\n        if first_words:\n            return Counter(first_words).most_common(1)[0][0].capitalize()\n        return \"Miscellaneous\"\n```\n\n### Phase 5: Build Complete Hierarchy\n```python\ndef phase5_build_hierarchy(df, cluster_mappings):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build complete 5-level hierarchy\n\n    For each row:\n    1. Map level 1 using level 1 clusters\n    2. Map level 2 based on level 1 assignment\n    3. Continue for all 5 levels\n    4. Ensure consistency (child must have parent)\n    \"\"\"\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        # Get mapping for this level\n        level_mapping = cluster_mappings.get(level, {})\n\n        # Apply mapping\n        df[f'unified_level_{level}'] = df['levels'].apply(\n            lambda x: level_mapping.get(x[level-1]) if len(x) >= level else None\n        )\n\n    # Ensure parent-child consistency\n    for level in range(2, 6):\n        for idx, row in df.iterrows():\n            if pd.notna(row[f'unified_level_{level}']):\n                # Ensure parent exists\n                for parent_level in range(1, level):\n                    if pd.isna(row[f'unified_level_{parent_level}']):\n                        # Fill in parent from other rows with same child\n                        parent = infer_parent(df, level, row[f'unified_level_{level}'])\n                        df.at[idx, f'unified_level_{parent_level}'] = parent\n\n    return df\n```\n\n### Phase 6: Validate and Export\n```python\ndef phase6_validate_export(df, output_dir):\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate taxonomy and export results\n\n    Validations:\n    1. Top level has 10-20 categories\n    2. Parent-child ratio is 3-10x\n    3. No parent-child overlap > 50%\n    4. Sibling overlap < 30%\n    5. Source distribution is balanced\n    \"\"\"\n    # Validation\n    validation_results = {\n        'level_counts': {},\n        'parent_child_ratios': {},\n        'source_distribution': {}\n    }\n\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        if col in df.columns:\n            validation_results['level_counts'][level] = df[col].nunique()\n\n    # Check source distribution\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        if col in df.columns:\n            dist = df.groupby(col)['source'].value_counts(normalize=True)\n            validation_results['source_distribution'][level] = dist\n\n    print(\"Validation Results:\")\n    print(f\"Level counts: {validation_results['level_counts']}\")\n\n    # Export unified_taxonomy_full.csv\n    output_cols = ['source', 'category_path', 'depth',\n                  'unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                  'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5']\n    df[output_cols].to_csv(f'{output_dir}/unified_taxonomy_full.csv',\n                           index=False)\n\n    # Export unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv (unique paths)\n    hierarchy_cols = ['unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                     'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5']\n    hierarchy_df = df[hierarchy_cols].drop_duplicates().sort_values(hierarchy_cols)\n    hierarchy_df.to_csv(f'{output_dir}/unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv',\n                       index=False)\n\n    return validation_results\n```\n\n## Complete Pipeline\n\n```python\ndef unify_taxonomies(source_files, output_dir, n_top_level=15):\n    \"\"\"\n    Complete taxonomy unification pipeline\n\n    Args:\n        source_files: Dict of {source_name: filepath}\n        output_dir: Output directory for results\n        n_top_level: Number of top-level categories\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with unified taxonomy\n    \"\"\"\n    # Phase 1: Normalize\n    print(\"Phase 1: Normalizing data...\")\n    df = phase1_normalize(source_files)\n\n    # Phase 2: Cluster\n    print(\"Phase 2: Clustering categories...\")\n    cluster_mappings = {}\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        cluster_mappings[level] = phase2_cluster_by_level(\n            df, level, n_top=n_top_level if level == 1 else None\n        )\n\n    # Phase 3: Refine\n    print(\"Phase 3: Refining clusters...\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        cluster_mappings[level] = phase3_refine_clusters(\n            cluster_mappings[level]\n        )\n\n    # Phase 4: Name\n    print(\"Phase 4: Naming categories...\")\n    for level in range(1, 6):\n        cluster_mappings[level] = {\n            k: phase4_name_categories(v)\n            for k, v in cluster_mappings[level].items()\n        }\n\n    # Phase 5: Build hierarchy\n    print(\"Phase 5: Building hierarchy...\")\n    df = phase5_build_hierarchy(df, cluster_mappings)\n\n    # Phase 6: Validate and export\n    print(\"Phase 6: Validating and exporting...\")\n    validation = phase6_validate_export(df, output_dir)\n\n    print(\"Taxonomy unification complete!\")\n    return df, validation\n```\n\n## Key Considerations\n\n### Balancing Trade-offs\n- Granularity vs Simplicity: Too many levels = complex, too few = loss of detail\n- Automation vs Manual Curation: Balance algorithmic clustering with domain knowledge\n- Source Fidelity vs Unification: Preserve source meaning while creating unified view\n\n### Common Pitfalls\n- Over-clustering: Creating too many small categories\n- Under-clustering: Merging semantically different categories\n- Ignoring frequency: Rare categories shouldn't dominate naming\n- Poor naming: Generic names like \"Other\" or \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n### Best Practices\n1. Start with top-level, work down\n2. Use multiple similarity metrics\n3. Validate at each level\n4. Iterate on cluster naming\n5. Check source distribution at each level\n6. Load CSV files from each source\n    2. Parse category paths into levels\n    3. Normalize text (case, punctuation, synonyms)\n    4. Count frequency of each category path\n    \"\"\"\n    import pandas as pd\n\n    all_data = []\n\n    for source, filepath in source_files.items():\n        df = pd.read_csv(filepath)\n        df['source'] = source\n\n        # Parse paths\n        df['levels'] = df['category_path'].apply(\n            lambda x: [normalize_text(level.strip())\n                      for level in x.split('>')]\n        )\n        df['depth'] = df['levels'].apply(len)\n\n        all_data.append(df)\n\n    combined = pd.concat(all_data, ignore_index=True)\n    return combined\n```\n\n### Phase 2: Build Initial Clusters at Each Level\n```python\ndef phase2_cluster_by_level(df, n_top=15):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster categories level by level\n\n    Level 1: Create 10-20 broad clusters using TF-IDF + K-Means\n    Level 2-5: Within each parent cluster, create subclusters\n    \"\"\"\n    from sklearn.cluster import KMeans\n    from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\n\n    # Convert to string\n    text = str(text)\n\n    # Remove quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '').replace(\"'\", '')\n\n    # Replace ampersands\n    text = text.replace(' & ', ' and ')\n    text = text.replace('&', ' and ')\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n\n    # Strip and title case\n    text = text.strip().title()\n\n    return text\n\n\ndef clean_category_name(text):\n    \"\"\"Clean and standardize category name\"\"\"\n    # Remove common redundant words at the end\n    redundant = ['Products', 'Items', 'Accessories', 'Supplies', 'Goods', 'Equipment']\n    words = text.split()\n\n    if len(words) > 1 and words[-1] in redundant:\n        words = words[:-1]\n\n    return ' '.join(words)\n\n\ndef apply_synonyms(text):\n    \"\"\"Apply synonym mapping for standardization\"\"\"\n    synonyms = {\n        'Apparel': 'Clothing',\n        'Footwear': 'Shoes',\n        'Eyewear': 'Glasses',\n        'Beverage': 'Drink',\n        'Automobile': 'Automotive',\n        'Mobile': 'Phone',\n        'Laptop': 'Computer',\n    }\n\n    words = text.split()\n    normalized = []\n    for word in words:\n        normalized.append(synonyms.get(word, word))\n\n    return ' '.join(normalized)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Similarity and Clustering Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, max_words=5, min_coverage=0.25):\n    \"\"\"Name cluster based on most common representative words\"\"\"\n    if not categories or len(categories) == 0:\n        return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n    # Tokenize\n    all_words = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        words = str(cat).lower().split()\n        all_words.extend(words)\n\n    # Stop words\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on',\n                  'other', 'all', 'more', 'products', 'items', 'supplies'}\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n\n    # Filter meaningful words with good coverage\n    selected = []\n    for word, count in word_counts.most_common(30):\n        if word not in stop_words and len(word) > 2:\n            coverage = count / len(categories)\n            if coverage >= min_coverage:\n                selected.append(word.capitalize())\n                if len(selected) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n    if selected:\n        return ' | '.join(selected[:max_words])\n\n    # Fallback: use most common first word\n    first_words = [str(cat).split()[0] for cat in categories if str(cat).split()]\n    if first_words:\n        return Counter(first_words).most_common(1)[0][0].capitalize()\n\n    return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n\ndef cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters, random_state=42):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories using TF-IDF and K-Means\"\"\"\n    if len(categories) < n_clusters:\n        # If fewer categories than clusters, each is its own cluster\n        return {cat: i for i, cat in enumerate(categories)}\n\n    # Vectorize\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=100, stop_words='english',\n                                  min_df=1, max_df=0.95)\n    try:\n        X = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n    except:\n        # Fallback: simple word-based clustering\n        return {cat: hash(cat) % n_clusters for cat in categories}\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=random_state, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Create mapping\n    mapping = {cat: label for cat, label in zip(categories, labels)}\n\n    return mapping\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Hierarchical Taxonomy Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_level_clusters(df, level_num, parent_col=None, n_clusters=None,\n                         min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Build clusters for a specific taxonomy level\"\"\"\n\n    # Extract categories at this level\n    level_col = f'level_{level_num}'\n\n    if parent_col:\n        # Group by parent\n        parent_groups = df.groupby(parent_col)[level_col].apply(\n            lambda x: list(x.dropna().unique())\n        )\n\n        all_mappings = {}\n\n        for parent, cats in parent_groups.items():\n            if len(cats) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Determine number of subclusters\n            if n_clusters:\n                n_sub = min(n_clusters, len(cats))\n            else:\n                # Adaptive: aim for 5-15 subcategories per parent\n                n_sub = max(3, min(15, len(cats) // 3))\n\n            if len(cats) <= n_sub:\n                # Few categories, keep as is\n                for cat in cats:\n                    all_mappings[cat] = clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n            else:\n                # Cluster subcategories\n                cluster_labels = cluster_categories_tfidf(cats, n_sub)\n\n                # Group by cluster\n                clusters = defaultdict(list)\n                for cat, label in cluster_labels.items():\n                    clusters[label].append(cat)\n\n                # Name each cluster\n                for label, cluster_cats in clusters.items():\n                    cluster_name = name_cluster_by_common_words(cluster_cats)\n                    cluster_name = clean_category_name(cluster_name)\n\n                    for cat in cluster_cats:\n                        all_mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return all_mappings\n\n    else:\n        # Top level clustering\n        categories = df[level_col].dropna().unique()\n\n        if len(categories) <= n_clusters:\n            # Few categories\n            return {cat: clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n                   for cat in categories}\n\n        # Cluster\n        cluster_labels = cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters)\n\n        # Group by cluster\n        clusters = defaultdict(list)\n        for cat, label in cluster_labels.items():\n            clusters[label].append(cat)\n\n        # Name clusters\n        mappings = {}\n        for label, cluster_cats in clusters.items():\n            cluster_name = name_cluster_by_common_words(cluster_cats, max_words=3)\n            cluster_name = clean_category_name(cluster_name)\n\n            for cat in cluster_cats:\n                mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return mappings\n\n\ndef check_parent_overlap(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Check if child name overlaps too much with parent\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return False\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = set(str(child).lower().split())\n\n    if not parent_words or not child_words:\n        return False\n\n    overlap = len(parent_words & child_words)\n\n    # If child contains most/all parent words, remove them\n    if overlap >= len(parent_words) * 0.7:\n        return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef remove_parent_words(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Remove parent words from child category\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return child\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = str(child).split()\n\n    # Keep words not in parent\n    unique_words = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n\n    if unique_words:\n        return ' | '.join(unique_words)\n\n    return child\n\n\ndef check_sibling_overlap(siblings, max_overlap=0.3):\n    \"\"\"Check if siblings are distinct enough\"\"\"\n    problems = []\n\n    for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n            overlap = word_overlap(str(sib1), str(sib2))\n            if overlap > max_overlap:\n                problems.append((sib1, sib2, overlap))\n\n    return problems\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Unification Pipeline\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef load_and_parse_data(data_dir='/root/data'):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse all category data\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Extract individual levels\n    max_depth = 5\n    for i in range(max_depth):\n        combined[f'level_{i+1}'] = combined['levels'].apply(\n            lambda x: x[i] if len(x) > i else None\n        )\n\n    print(f\"Depth distribution:\")\n    print(combined['depth'].value_counts().sort_index())\n\n    return combined\n\n\ndef build_unified_taxonomy(df, n_top_level=15):\n    \"\"\"Build unified taxonomy across all levels\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*70)\n    print(\"Building Unified Taxonomy\")\n    print(\"=\"*70)\n\n    # Remove common redundant words at the end\n    redundant = ['Products', 'Items', 'Accessories', 'Supplies', 'Goods', 'Equipment']\n    words = text.split()\n\n    if len(words) > 1 and words[-1] in redundant:\n        words = words[:-1]\n\n    return ' '.join(words)\n\n\ndef apply_synonyms(text):\n    \"\"\"Apply synonym mapping for standardization\"\"\"\n    synonyms = {\n        'Apparel': 'Clothing',\n        'Footwear': 'Shoes',\n        'Eyewear': 'Glasses',\n        'Beverage': 'Drink',\n        'Automobile': 'Automotive',\n        'Mobile': 'Phone',\n        'Laptop': 'Computer',\n    }\n\n    words = text.split()\n    normalized = []\n    for word in words:\n        normalized.append(synonyms.get(word, word))\n\n    return ' '.join(normalized)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Similarity and Clustering Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, max_words=5, min_coverage=0.25):\n    \"\"\"Name cluster based on most common representative words\"\"\"\n    if not categories or len(categories) == 0:\n        return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n    # Tokenize\n    all_words = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        words = str(cat).lower().split()\n        all_words.extend(words)\n\n    # Stop words\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on',\n                  'other', 'all', 'more', 'products', 'items', 'supplies'}\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n\n    # Filter meaningful words with good coverage\n    selected = []\n    for word, count in word_counts.most_common(30):\n        if word not in stop_words and len(word) > 2:\n            coverage = count / len(categories)\n            if coverage >= min_coverage:\n                selected.append(word.capitalize())\n                if len(selected) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n    if selected:\n        return ' | '.join(selected[:max_words])\n\n    # Fallback: use most common first word\n    first_words = [str(cat).split()[0] for cat in categories if str(cat).split()]\n    if first_words:\n        return Counter(first_words).most_common(1)[0][0].capitalize()\n\n    return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n\ndef cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters, random_state=42):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories using TF-IDF and K-Means\"\"\"\n    if len(categories) < n_clusters:\n        # If fewer categories than clusters, each is its own cluster\n        return {cat: i for i, cat in enumerate(categories)}\n\n    # Vectorize\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=100, stop_words='english',\n                                  min_df=1, max_df=0.95)\n    try:\n        X = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n    except:\n        # Fallback: simple word-based clustering\n        return {cat: hash(cat) % n_clusters for cat in categories}\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=random_state, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Create mapping\n    mapping = {cat: label for cat, label in zip(categories, labels)}\n\n    return mapping\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Hierarchical Taxonomy Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_level_clusters(df, level_num, parent_col=None, n_clusters=None,\n                         min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Build clusters for a specific taxonomy level\"\"\"\n\n    # Extract categories at this level\n    level_col = f'level_{level_num}'\n\n    if parent_col:\n        # Group by parent\n        parent_groups = df.groupby(parent_col)[level_col].apply(\n            lambda x: list(x.dropna().unique())\n        )\n\n        all_mappings = {}\n\n        for parent, cats in parent_groups.items():\n            if len(cats) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Determine number of subclusters\n            if n_clusters:\n                n_sub = min(n_clusters, len(cats))\n            else:\n                # Adaptive: aim for 5-15 subcategories per parent\n                n_sub = max(3, min(15, len(cats) // 3))\n\n            if len(cats) <= n_sub:\n                # Few categories, keep as is\n                for cat in cats:\n                    all_mappings[cat] = clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n            else:\n                # Cluster subcategories\n                cluster_labels = cluster_categories_tfidf(cats, n_sub)\n\n                # Group by cluster\n                clusters = defaultdict(list)\n                for cat, label in cluster_labels.items():\n                    clusters[label].append(cat)\n\n                # Name each cluster\n                for label, cluster_cats in clusters.items():\n                    cluster_name = name_cluster_by_common_words(cluster_cats)\n                    cluster_name = clean_category_name(cluster_name)\n\n                    for cat in cluster_cats:\n                        all_mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return all_mappings\n\n    else:\n        # Top level clustering\n        categories = df[level_col].dropna().unique()\n\n        if len(categories) <= n_clusters:\n            # Few categories\n            return {cat: clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n                   for cat in categories}\n\n        # Cluster\n        cluster_labels = cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters)\n\n        # Group by cluster\n        clusters = defaultdict(list)\n        for cat, label in cluster_labels.items():\n            clusters[label].append(cat)\n\n        # Name clusters\n        mappings = {}\n        for label, cluster_cats in clusters.items():\n            cluster_name = name_cluster_by_common_words(cluster_cats, max_words=3)\n            cluster_name = clean_category_name(cluster_name)\n\n            for cat in cluster_cats:\n                mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return mappings\n\n\ndef check_parent_overlap(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Check if child name overlaps too much with parent\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return False\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = set(str(child).lower().split())\n\n    if not parent_words or not child_words:\n        return False\n\n    overlap = len(parent_words & child_words)\n\n    # If child contains most/all parent words, remove them\n    if overlap >= len(parent_words) * 0.7:\n        return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef remove_parent_words(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Remove parent words from child category\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return child\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = str(child).split()\n\n    # Keep words not in parent\n    unique_words = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n\n    if unique_words:\n        return ' | '.join(unique_words)\n\n    return child\n\n\ndef check_sibling_overlap(siblings, max_overlap=0.3):\n    \"\"\"Check if siblings are distinct enough\"\"\"\n    problems = []\n\n    for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n            overlap = word_overlap(str(sib1), str(sib2))\n            if overlap > max_overlap:\n                problems.append((sib1, sib2, overlap))\n\n    return problems\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Unification Pipeline\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef load_and_parse_data(data_dir='/root/data'):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse all category data\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    amazon_df = pd.read_csv(f'{data_dir}/amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_df['source'] = 'amazon'\n\n    # Load Facebook\n    fb_df = pd.read_csv(f'{data_dir}/fb_product_categories.csv')\n    fb_df['source'] = 'facebook'\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    google_df = pd.read_csv(f'{data_dir}/google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_df['source'] = 'google'\n\n    # Combine\n    all_dfs = []\n    for df in [amazon_df, fb_df, google_df]:\n        # Ensure category_path column exists\n        if 'category_path' in df.columns:\n            all_dfs.append(df[['source', 'category_path']])\n\n    combined = pd.concat(all_dfs, ignore_index=True)\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(combined)} category paths from {combined['source'].nunique()} sources\")\n\n    # Parse into levels\n    print(\"Parsing category hierarchies...\")\n    combined['levels'] = combined['category_path'].apply(\n        lambda x: [normalize_text(level.strip()) for level in str(x).split('>')]\n    )\n    combined['depth'] = combined['levels'].apply(len)\n\n    # Extract individual levels\n    max_depth = 5\n    for i in range(max_depth):\n        combined[f'level_{i+1}'] = combined['levels'].apply(\n            lambda x: x[i] if len(x) > i else None\n        )\n\n    print(f\"Depth distribution:\")\n    print(combined['depth'].value_counts().sort_index())\n\n    return combined\n\n\ndef build_unified_taxonomy(df, n_top_level=15):\n    \"\"\"Build unified taxonomy across all levels\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*70)\n    print(\"Building Unified Taxonomy\")\n    print(\"=\"*70)\n\n    # Adaptive\n    )\n\n    df['unified_level_2'] = df['level_2'].map(level_2_mapping)\n\n    # Remove parent overlap\n    for idx, row in df.iterrows():\n        if pd.notna(row['unified_level_1']) and pd.notna(row['unified_level_2']):\n            if check_parent_overlap(row['unified_level_1'], row['unified_level_2']):\n                df.at[idx, 'unified_level_2'] = remove_parent_words(\n                    row['unified_level_1'],\n                    row['unified_level_2']\n                )\n\n    print(f\"  Created {df['unified_level_2'].nunique()} unique level 2 categories\")\n\n    # Convert to string\n    text = str(text)\n\n    # Remove quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '').replace(\"'\", '')\n\n    # Replace ampersands\n    text = text.replace(' & ', ' and ')\n    text = clean_category_name(text):\n    \"\"\"Clean and standardize category name\"\"\"\n    # Remove common redundant words at the end\n    redundant = ['Products', 'Items', 'Accessories', 'Supplies', \"\"\"Apply synonym mapping for standardization\"\"\"\n    synonyms = {\n        'Apparel': 'Clothing',\n        'Footwear': 'Shoes',\n        'Eyewear': 'Glasses',\n        'Beverage': 'Drink',\n        'Automobile': 'Automotive',\n        'Mobile': 'Phone',\n        'Laptop': 'Computer',\n    }\n\n    words = text.split()\n    normalized = []\n    for word in words:\n set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, max_words=5, min_coverage=0.25):\n    \"\"\"Name cluster based on most common representative words\"\"\"\n    if not categories or len(categories) == 0:\n stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on',\n                  'other', 'all', 'more', 'products', 'items', 'supplies'}\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = for cat, label in zip(categories, labels)}\n\n    return mapping\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Hierarchical Taxonomy Building\n#             all_mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return all_mappings\n\n    else:\n        # Top level clustering\n        categories = df[level_col].dropna().unique()\n\n        if normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category text\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Convert to string\n    text = str(text)\n\n    # Remove quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '').replace(\"'\", '')\n\n    # Replace ampersands\n    text = text.replace(' & ', ' and ')\n    text = text.replace('&', ' and ')\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n\n    # Strip and title case\n    text = text.strip().title()\n\n    return text\n\n\ndef clean_category_name(text):\n    \"\"\"Clean and standardize category name\"\"\"\n    # Remove common redundant words at the end\n    redundant = ['Products', 'Items', 'Accessories', 'Supplies', 'Goods', 'Equipment']\n    words = text.split()\n\n    if len(words) > 1 and words[-1] in redundant:\n        words = words[:-1]\n\n    return ' '.join(words)\n\n\ndef apply_synonyms(text):\n    \"\"\"Apply synonym mapping for standardization\"\"\"\n    synonyms = {\n        'Apparel': 'Clothing',\n        'Footwear': 'Shoes',\n        'Eyewear': 'Glasses',\n        'Beverage': 'Drink',\n        'Automobile': 'Automotive',\n        'Mobile': 'Phone',\n        'Laptop': 'Computer',\n    }\n\n    words = text.split()\n    normalized = []\n    for word in words:\n        normalized.append(synonyms.get(word, word))\n\n    return ' '.join(normalized)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Similarity and Clustering Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on',\n                  'other', 'all', 'more', 'products', 'items', 'supplies'}\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n\n    # Filter meaningful words with good coverage\n    selected = []\n    for word, count in word_counts.most_common(30):\n        if word not in stop_words and len(word) > 2:\n            coverage = count / len(categories)\n            if coverage >= min_coverage:\n                selected.append(word.capitalize())\n                if len(selected) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n    if selected:\n        return ' | '.join(selected[:max_words])\n\n    # Fallback: use most common first word\n    first_words = [str(cat).split()[0] for cat in categories if str(cat).split()]\n    if first_words:\n        return Counter(first_words).most_common(1)[0][0].capitalize()\n\n    return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n\ndef cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters, random_state=42):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories using TF-IDF and K-Means\"\"\"\n    if len(categories) < n_clusters:\n        # If fewer categories than clusters, each is its own cluster\n        return {cat: i for i, cat in enumerate(categories)}\n\n    # Vectorize\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=100, stop_words='english',\n                                  min_df=1, max_df=0.95)\n    try:\n        X = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n    except:\n        # Fallback: simple word-based clustering\n        return {cat: hash(cat) % n_clusters for cat in categories}\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=random_state, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Create mapping\n    mapping = {cat: label for cat, label in zip(categories, labels)}\n\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef keyword_based_top_level_clustering(categories):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster top-level categories using domain knowledge keywords\n    This provides better initial clustering than pure TF-IDF\n    \"\"\"\n    keyword_clusters = {\n        'Clothing | Apparel | Fashion': ['clothing', 'apparel', 'fashion', 'shoes', 'jewelry', 'watches', 'accessories', 'mens', 'womens', 'kids', 'girls', 'boys'],\n        'Electronics | Computers | Tech': ['electronics', 'computers', 'software', 'hardware', 'cameras', 'audio', 'video', 'phones', 'cell', 'mobile', 'tablets', ['tools', 'improvement', 'hardware', 'building', 'paint', 'plumbing', 'electrical'],\n        'Industrial | Scientific | Business': ['industrial', 'scientific', 'business', 'office', 'lab', 'medical', 'safety', 'packaging'],\n        'Media | Books | Music': ['books', 'music', 'movies', 'cds', 'vinyl', 'dvd', 'blu-ray', 'kindle'],\n        'Luggage | Travel': ['luggage', 'travel', 'bags', 'suitcase', 'backpack', 'carry-on']\n    }\n\n    # Create mapping\n    category_mapping = {}\n    unmatched = []\n\n    for cat in categories:\n        cat_lower = str(cat).lower()\n        matched = False\n\n        # Try to match with keyword clusters\n        for cluster_name, keywords in keyword_clusters.items():\n            if any(keyword in cat_lower for keyword in keywords):\n                category_mapping[cat] = cluster_name\n                matched = True\n                break\n\n        if not matched:\n            unmatched.append(cat)\n\n    # Handle unmatched categories - assign to \"Other\"\n    for cat in unmatched:\n        category_mapping[cat] = 'Other | Miscellaneous'\n\n    return category_mapping\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Hierarchical Taxonomy Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_level_clusters(df, level_num, parent_col=None, n_clusters=None,\n                         min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Build clusters for a specific taxonomy level\"\"\"\n\n    # Extract categories at this level\n    level_col = f'level_{level_num}'\n\n    if parent_col:\n        # Group by parent\n        parent_groups = df.groupby(parent_col)[level_col].apply(\n            lambda x: list(x.dropna().unique())\n        )\n\n        all_mappings = {}\n\n        for parent, cats in parent_groups.items():\n            if len(cats) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Determine number of subclusters\n            if n_clusters:\n                n_sub = min(n_clusters, len(cats))\n            else:\n                # Adaptive: aim for 5-15 subcategories per parent\n                n_sub = max(3, min(15, len(cats) // 3))\n\n            if len(cats) <= n_sub:\n                # Few categories, keep as is\n                for cat in cats:\n                    all_mappings[cat] = clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n            else:\n                # Cluster subcategories\n                cluster_labels = cluster_categories_tfidf(cats, n_sub)\n\n                # Group by cluster\n                clusters = defaultdict(list)\n                for cat, label in cluster_labels.items():\n all_mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return all_mappings\n\n    else:\n        # Top level clustering\n        categories = df[level_col].dropna().unique()\n\n        if len(categories) <= n_clusters:\n            # Few categories\n            return {cat: clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n                   for cat in categories}\n\n        # Cluster\n return False\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = set(str(child).lower().split())\n\n    if not parent_words or not child_words:\n        return False\n\n    overlap = len(parent_words & child_words)\n\n    # If child contains most/all parent words, remove them\n    if overlap >= len(parent_words) * 0.7:\n        return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef remove_parent_words(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Remove parent words from child category\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return child\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = str(child).split()\n\n    # Keep words not in parent\n    unique_words = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n\n    if unique_words:\n        return ' | '.join(unique_words)\n\n    return child\n\n\ndef check_sibling_overlap(siblings, max_overlap=0.3):\n    \"\"\"Check if siblings are distinct enough\"\"\"\n    problems = []\n\n    for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n            overlap = word_overlap(str(sib1), str(sib2))\n            if overlap > max_overlap:\n                problems.append((sib1, sib2, overlap))\n\n    return problems\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Unification Pipeline\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef load_and_parse_data(data_dir='/root/data'):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse all category data\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading Script\n\nThis script unifies product taxonomies from Amazon, Facebook, and Google Shopping\ninto a single 5-level hierarchical taxonomy.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport
