============================================================================\n\ndef normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category text\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Convert to string\n    text = str(text)\n\n    # Remove quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '').replace(\"'\", '')\n\n    # Replace ampersands\n    text = text.replace(' & ', ' and ')\n    text = text.replace('&', ' and ')\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n\n    # Strip and title case\n    text = text.strip().title()\n\n    return text\n\n\ndef clean_category_name(text):\n    \"\"\"Clean and standardize category name\"\"\"\n    # Remove common redundant words at the end\n    redundant = ['Products', 'Items', 'Accessories', 'Supplies', 'Goods', 'Equipment']\n    words = text.split()\n\n    if len(words) > 1 and words[-1] in redundant:\n        words = words[:-1]\n\n    return ' '.join(words)\n\n\ndef apply_synonyms(text):\n    \"\"\"Apply synonym mapping for standardization\"\"\"\n    synonyms = {\n        'Apparel': 'Clothing',\n        'Footwear': 'Shoes',\n        'Eyewear': 'Glasses',\n        'Beverage': 'Drink',\n        'Automobile': 'Automotive',\n        'Mobile': 'Phone',\n        'Laptop': 'Computer',\n    }\n\n    words = text.split()\n    normalized = []\n    for word in words:\n        normalized.append(synonyms.get(word, word))\n\n    return ' '.join(normalized)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Similarity and Clustering Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef jaccard_similarity(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity between two strings\"\"\"\n    set1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    set2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n    union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef word_overlap(str1, str2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(str1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(str2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = len(words1 & words2)\n    min_length = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return intersection / min_length\n\n\ndef name_cluster_by_common_words(categories, max_words=5, min_coverage=0.25):\n    \"\"\"Name cluster based on most common representative words\"\"\"\n    if not categories or len(categories) == 0:\n        return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n    # Tokenize\n    all_words = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        words = str(cat).lower().split()\n        all_words.extend(words)\n\n    # Stop words\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'to', 'in', 'on',\n                  'other', 'all', 'more', 'products', 'items', 'supplies'}\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n\n    # Filter meaningful words with good coverage\n    selected = []\n    for word, count in word_counts.most_common(30):\n        if word not in stop_words and len(word) > 2:\n            coverage = count / len(categories)\n            if coverage >= min_coverage:\n                selected.append(word.capitalize())\n                if len(selected) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n    if selected:\n        return ' | '.join(selected[:max_words])\n\n    # Fallback: use most common first word\n    first_words = [str(cat).split()[0] for cat in categories if str(cat).split()]\n    if first_words:\n        return Counter(first_words).most_common(1)[0][0].capitalize()\n\n    return \"Miscellaneous\"\n\n\ndef cluster_categories_tfidf(categories, n_clusters, random_state=42):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories using TF-IDF and K-Means\"\"\"\n    if len(categories) < n_clusters:\n        # If fewer categories than clusters, each is its own cluster\n        return {cat: i for i, cat in enumerate(categories)}\n\n    # Vectorize\n    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=100, stop_words='english',\n                                  min_df=1, max_df=0.95)\n    try:\n        X = vectorizer.fit_transform(categories)\n    except:\n        # Fallback: simple word-based clustering\n        return {cat: hash(cat) % n_clusters for cat in categories}\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=random_state, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)\n\n    # Create mapping\n    mapping = {cat: label for cat, label in zip(categories, labels)}\n\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef keyword_based_top_level_clustering(categories):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cluster top-level categories using domain knowledge 'Luggage | Travel': ['luggage', 'travel', 'bags', 'suitcase', 'backpack', 'carry-on']\n    }\n\n    # Create mapping\n    category_mapping = {}\n    unmatched = []\n\n    for cat in categories:\n unmatched.append(cat)\n\n    # Handle unmatched categories - assign to \"Other\"\n    for cat in unmatched:\n        category_mapping[cat] = 'Other | Miscellaneous'\n\n    return ============================================================================\n\ndef build_level_clusters(df, level_num, parent_col=None, n_clusters=None,\n                         min_size=3, parent_groups.items():\n            if len(cats) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Determine number of subclusters\n            if n_clusters:\n                n_sub = min(n_clusters,         # Few categories, keep as is\n                for cat in cats:\n                    all_mappings[cat] = clean_category_name(apply_synonyms(normalize_text(cat)))\n            else:\n             all_mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return all_mappings\n\n    else:\n        # Top level clustering - use keyword-based approach\n        categories = mappings[cat] = cluster_name\n\n        return mappings\n\n\ndef check_parent_overlap(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Check if child name overlaps too much with parent\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return False\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = set(str(child).lower().split())\n\n    if not parent_words or not child_words:\n        return False\n\n    overlap = len(parent_words & child_words)\n\n    # If child contains most/all parent words, remove them\n    if overlap >= len(parent_words) * 0.7:\n        return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef remove_parent_words(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Remove parent words from child category\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return child\n\n    parent_words = set(str(parent).lower().split())\n    child_words = str(child).split()\n\n    # Keep words not in parent\n    unique_words = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n\n    if unique_words:\n        return ' | '.join(unique_words)\n\n    return child\n\n\ndef check_sibling_overlap(siblings, max_overlap=0.3):\n    \"\"\"Check if siblings are distinct enough\"\"\"\n    problems = []\n\n    for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n            overlap = word_overlap(str(sib1), str(sib2))\n            if overlap > max_overlap:\n                problems.append((sib1, sib2, overlap))\n\n    return problems\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Unification Pipeline\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef load_and_parse_data(data_dir='/root/data'):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse all category data\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    # Load Amazon\n    amazon_df = pd.read_csv(f'{data_dir}/amazon_product_categories.csv')\n    amazon_df['source'] = 'amazon'\n\n    # Load Facebook\n    fb_df = pd.read_csv(f'{data_dir}/fb_product_categories.csv')\n    fb_df['source'] = 'facebook'\n\n    # Load Google Shopping\n    google_df = pd.read_csv(f'{data_dir}/google_shopping_product_categories.csv')\n    google_df['source'] = 'google'\n\n    # Combine\n    all_dfs = []\n    for df in [amazon_df, fb_df, google_df]:\n        # Ensure category_path column exists\n        if 'category_path' in df.columns:\n            all_dfs.append(df[['source', 'category_path']])\n\n    combined = pd.concat(all_dfs, ignore_index=True)\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(combined)} category paths from {combined['source'].nunique()} sources\")\n\n    # Parse into levels\n    print(\"Parsing category hierarchies...\")\n    combined['levels'] = combined['category_path'].apply(\n        lambda x: [normalize_text(level.strip()) for level in str(x).split('>')]\n    )\n    combined['depth'] = combined['levels'].apply(len)\n\n    # Extract individual levels\n    max_depth = 5\n    for i in range(max_depth):\n        combined[f'level_{i+1}'] = combined['levels'].apply(\n            lambda x: x[i] if len(x) > i else None\n        )\n\n Category Naming\n- Extracted most common representative words from cluster members\n- Used pipe separator \" | \" between words\n- Limited to maximum 5 words per category name\n- Ensured 70%+ representativeness of subcategories\n\n### 4.
